\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbb{P}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\left\{X_{t}\notin\mathcal{E}_{t}\text{ and }\|X_{t}\|_{2}\leq T^{2c_{R}+2}\right\}+\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbb{P}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\left\{\|X_{t}\|_{2}>T^{2c_{R}+2}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\displaystyle\int}_{x_{t}:q_{t}(x_{t})\leq\exp(-c_{6}d\log T),\|x_{t}\|_{2}\leq T^{2c_{R}+2}}q_{t}(x_{t})\mathrm{d}x_{t}+T\exp\big(-c_{6}d\log T\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq T\big(2T^{2c_{R}+2}\big)^{d}\exp(-c_{6}d\log T)+T\exp\big(-c_{6}d\log T\big)\leq\exp\Big(-\frac{c_{6}}{2}d\log T\Big),

where the penultimate line comes from (
62
), and
the last inequality holds true as long as
c_{6}
is large enough.
Plugging the preceding two results into (
152
), we reach

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{Y_{T}\sim p_{T}}\bigg[\frac{q_{1}(Y_{1})}{p_{1}(Y_{1})}\ind\left\{Y_{1}\in\mathcal{E},Y_{T}\in\mathcal{I}_{4}\right\}\bigg]\leq\frac{8}{7}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{Y_{T}\sim p_{T}}\big[(h_{t}-h_{t-1})\ind\left\{y_{t}\in\mathcal{E}_{t},Y_{T}\in\mathcal{J}_{1,t}\right\}\big]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\qquad\qquad+\frac{8}{7}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{Y_{T}\sim p_{T}}\big[h_{t}\ind\left\{Y_{T}\in\mathcal{J}_{2,t}\right\}\big]+\exp\Big(-\frac{c_{6}}{2}d\log T\Big).

(154)

As it turns out, the sum w.r.t. the set
\mathcal{J}_{1,t}
and the sum w.r.t. the set
\mathcal{J}_{2,t}

in (
154
) can be controlled respectively using the same arguments as for
\mathcal{I}_{1}
and
\mathcal{I}_{3}
to derive

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{Y_{T}\sim p_{T}}\big[(h_{t}-h_{t-1})\ind\left\{y_{t}\in\mathcal{E}_{t},Y_{T}\in\mathcal{J}_{1,t}\right\}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d^{2}\log^{4}T}{T}+\frac{d^{6}\log^{6}T}{T^{2}}+\sqrt{d\log^{3}T}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score}}+(d\log T)\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi}},

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{Y_{T}\sim p_{T}}\big[h_{t}\ind\left\{Y_{T}\in\mathcal{J}_{2,t}\right\}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{d\log^{3}T}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score}}+(d\log T)\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi}};

we omit the arguments here for the sake of brevity.
Therefore, we have proven that

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathbb{E}}_{Y_{T}\sim p_{T}}\bigg[\frac{q_{1}(Y_{1})}{p_{1}(Y_{1})}\ind\left\{Y_{1}\in\mathcal{E},Y_{T}\in\mathcal{I}_{4}\right\}\bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d^{2}\log^{4}T}{T}+\frac{d^{6}\log^{6}T}{T^{2}}+\sqrt{d\log^{3}T}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score}}+(d\log T)\varepsilon_{\mathsf{Jacobi}}.

(155)

Putting all this together.

Taking (
147
), (
149
) and (
155
) together, we establish the advertised result.

Appendix C
Proofs of auxiliary lemmas for the DDPM-type sampler

C.1
Proof of Lemma
8

For notational simplicity, we find it helpful to define, for any constant
\gamma\in[0,1]
,

\displaystyle x_{t}(\gamma)\coloneqq\gamma x_{t-1}+(1-\gamma)\widehat{x}_{t}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\widehat{x}_{t}\coloneqq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}x_{t}.

(156)

Step 1: decomposing the target distribution
p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})
.

With this piece of notation in mind, we can recall the forward process (
4
) and calculate:
for any
x_{t-1},x_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}p_{X_{t-1},X_{t}}(x_{t-1},x_{t})=\frac{1}{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}\exp\Big(\log p_{X_{t-1}}(x_{t-1})+\log p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{t-1})\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}\exp\bigg(\log p_{X_{t-1}}(\widehat{x}_{t})+\int_{0}^{1}\Big[\nabla\log p_{X_{t-1}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\big)\Big]^{\top}(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})\mathrm{d}\gamma+\log p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{t-1})\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{X_{t-1}}(\widehat{x}_{t})}{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}\exp\bigg((x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}\gamma\int_{x_{0}}\frac{\nabla p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\,|\,x_{0}\big)p_{X_{0}}(x_{0})}{p_{X_{t-1}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\big)}\mathrm{d}x_{0}+\log p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{t-1})\bigg),

(157)

where the penultimate line comes from the fundamental theorem of calculus.
In particular, the exponent in (
157
) consists of a term that satisfies

\displaystyle(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}\gamma\int_{x_{0}}\frac{\nabla p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\,|\,x_{0}\big)p_{X_{0}}(x_{0})}{p_{X_{t-1}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\big)}\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle=(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}\gamma\int_{x_{0}}\frac{\nabla p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\,|\,x_{0}\big)}{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\,|\,x_{0}\big)}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}(\gamma)\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle=-(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}\gamma\int_{x_{0}}\frac{x_{t}(\gamma)-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}(\gamma)\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle\eqqcolon-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}\int_{0}^{1}g_{t-1}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\big)\mathrm{d}\gamma,

(158)

where the second line holds since

p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}(\gamma)\big)p_{X_{t-1}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\big)=p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\,|\,x_{0}\big)p_{X_{0}}\big(x_{0}\big)
,
and we remind the reader of the definition of
g_{t}(\cdot)
in (
36
).

To continue, it is then seen from the fundamental
theorem of calculus that

\displaystyle g_{t-1}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\big)

\displaystyle=g_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)+\int_{0}^{1}J_{t-1}\Big((1-\tau)\widehat{x}_{t}+\tau x_{t}(\gamma)\Big)\big(x_{t}(\gamma)-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\mathrm{d}\tau,

where
J_{t-1}(x)\coloneqq\frac{\partial g_{t-1}(x)}{\partial x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}

is the associated Jacobian matrix.
As a consequence, we can show that

\displaystyle(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}\gamma\int_{x_{0}}\frac{\nabla p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\,|\,x_{0}\big)p_{X_{0}}(x_{0})}{p_{X_{t-1}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\big)}\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\left\{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)+(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}J_{t-1}\Big((1-\tau)\widehat{x}_{t}+\tau x_{t}(\gamma)\Big)\big(x_{t}(\gamma)-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\mathrm{d}\tau\mathrm{d}\gamma\right\}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\left\{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)+(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\gamma J_{t-1}\Big((1-\tau)\widehat{x}_{t}+\tau x_{t}(\gamma)\Big)\mathrm{d}\tau\mathrm{d}\gamma\right]\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\right\}.

(159)

Combining (
157
) and (
159
) allows us to rewrite the target quantity
p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})
as:

\displaystyle p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{X_{t-1}}(\widehat{x}_{t})}{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}\exp\Bigg((x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}\int_{0}^{1}\mathrm{d}\gamma\int_{x_{0}}\frac{\nabla p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}(\widetilde{x}_{t}\,|\,x_{0})p_{X_{0}}(x_{0})}{p_{X_{t-1}}(\widetilde{x}_{t})}\mathrm{d}x_{0}+\log p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{t-1})\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{X_{t-1}}(\widehat{x}_{t})}{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}\exp\Bigg(-\frac{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)+(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\gamma J_{t-1}\Big((1-\tau)\widehat{x}_{t}+\tau x_{t}(\gamma)\Big)\mathrm{d}\tau\mathrm{d}\gamma\right]\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{\alpha_{t}\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}-\frac{d}{2}\log\big(2\pi(1-\alpha)\big)\Bigg),

(160)

where we have also used the fact that conditional on
X_{t}\,|\,X_{t-1}=x_{t-1}\sim\mathcal{N}\big(\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{t-1},(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})I_{d}\big).

Note that the pre-factor
\frac{p_{X_{t-1}}(\widehat{x}_{t})}{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}
in the above display is independent from the specific value of
x_{t-1}
.

Step 2: controlling the exponent in (
160
).

Consider now any
(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}
(cf. (
80
)).
In order to further simplify the exponent in the display (
160
),
we make the following claims:

(a)

for any
x
that can be written as
x=wx_{t-1}+(1-w)x_{t}/\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}
for some
w\in[0,1]
,
the Jacobian matrix
J_{t-1}(x)=\frac{\partial g_{t-1}(x)}{\partial x}
obeys

\displaystyle\big\|J_{t-1}(x)-I\big\|\lesssim d\log T;

(161a)

(b)

in addition, one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big\|\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)g_{t}(x_{t})-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)g_{t}(x_{t})\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d\log^{2}T}{T^{3/2}}.

(161b)

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{g_{t-1}(\widehat{x}_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}-\frac{g_{t}(x_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1-\alpha_{t})\Big(\frac{d\log T}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{3/2},

(161c)

Assuming the validity of these claims (which will be established in Appendix
C.1.1
) and recalling the definition of
\mu_{t}(\cdot)
in (
74b
), we can use (
160
) together with a little algebra to obtain

\displaystyle p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})

\displaystyle=f_{0}(x_{t})\exp\bigg(-\frac{\alpha_{t}\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}+\zeta_{t}(x_{t-1},x_{t})\bigg)

(162)

for some function
f_{0}(\cdot)
and some residual term
\zeta_{t}(x_{t-1},x_{t})
obeying

\displaystyle|\zeta_{t}(x_{t-1},x_{t})|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big\|_{2}^{2}\sup_{x:\,\exists w\in[0,1]\text{ s.t. }x=(1-w)\widehat{x}_{t}+wx_{t-1}}\big\|J_{t-1}(x)\big\|

\displaystyle\quad+\big\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big\|_{2}\Bigg\|\frac{\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t})(\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0})\mathrm{d}x_{0}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}-\frac{\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})\mathrm{d}x_{0}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\big(d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T\big)d\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}+\sqrt{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}\,(1-\alpha_{t})\bigg(\frac{d\log T}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg)^{3/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})d^{2}\log^{2}T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}+d^{2}\bigg(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg)^{3/2}\log^{2}T\asymp\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})d^{2}\log^{2}T}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}},

where the penultimate line makes use of the assumption
(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}
(cf. (
80
)),
and the last inequality holds since
\alpha_{t}\geq 1/2
(cf. (
22
)).

Step 3: approximating the function
f_{0}(x_{t})
.

To finish up, it remains to quantify the function
f_{0}(\cdot)
in (
162
).
Note that for any
x_{t}
obeying
p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\geq\exp\big(-\frac{1}{2}c_{6}d\log T\big)
,
it is easily seen that

\displaystyle\int_{x_{t-1}:(x_{t},x_{t-1})\notin\mathcal{E}}p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}=\frac{\int_{x_{t-1}:(x_{t},x_{t-1})\notin\mathcal{E}}p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{t-1})p_{X_{t-1}}(x_{t-1})\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}}{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{\frac{1}{(2\pi(1-\alpha_{t}))^{d/2}}\int_{x_{t-1}:(x_{t},x_{t-1})\notin\mathcal{E}}\exp\big(-\frac{\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{t-1}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}}{\exp\big(-\frac{1}{2}c_{6}d\log T\big)}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{\exp\big(-c_{3}d\log T\big)}{\exp\big(-\frac{1}{2}c_{6}d\log T\big)}\leq\exp\Big(-\frac{1}{4}c_{6}d\log T\Big),

provided that
c_{3}\geq 3c_{6}/4
.
This means that

1\geq\int_{x_{t-1}:(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}}p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\geq 1-\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4}c_{6}d\log T\right).

(163)

Moreover, for any
(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}
, one has

\displaystyle\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\bigg\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\mathbb{E}\Big[x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\mid X_{t}=x_{t}\Big]\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\big\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big\|_{2}-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}\mid X_{t}=x_{t}\Big]

\displaystyle\geq c_{3}\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\cdot\sqrt{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}6\overline{c}_{5}\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}c_{3}\sqrt{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T},

where the first identity comes from the definition (
74b
)
of
\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})
, the penultimate line makes use of the result
(
43a
) in Lemma
1
, and the last inequality
is valid as long as
c_{3}
is sufficiently large.
This in turn allows one to derive

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big(2\pi\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\big)^{d/2}}\int_{x_{t-1}:(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}}\exp\bigg(-\frac{\alpha_{t}\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\bigg)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\geq 1-\exp\big(-c_{3}d\log T\big).

(164)

In addition, by virtue of (
162
), the integral in (
163
) can be respectively bounded from above and from below as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{x_{t-1}:(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}}p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{f_{0}(x_{t})\int_{x_{t-1}:(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}+\zeta_{t}(x_{t-1},x_{t})\Big)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}}{\big(2\pi\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\big)^{-d/2}\int_{x_{t-1}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\Big)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{f_{0}(x_{t})\int_{x_{t-1}:(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}+\zeta_{t}(x_{t-1},x_{t})\Big)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}}{\big(2\pi\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\big)^{-d/2}\int_{x_{t-1}:(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\Big)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{f_{0}(x_{t})}{\big(2\pi\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\big)^{-d/2}}\exp\Bigg\{O\bigg(d^{2}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)\log^{2}T\bigg)\Bigg\}

and

\displaystyle\int_{x_{t-1}:(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}}p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}

\displaystyle\quad\geq\big(1-\exp\left(-c_{3}d\log T\right)\big)\frac{f_{0}(x_{t})\int_{x_{t-1}:(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}+\zeta_{t}(x_{t-1},x_{t})\Big)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}}{\big(2\pi\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\big)^{-d/2}\int_{x_{t-1}:(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\Big)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\quad\geq\big(1-\exp\left(-c_{3}d\log T\right)\big)\frac{f_{0}(x_{t})}{\big(2\pi\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\big)^{-d/2}}\exp\Bigg\{-O\bigg(d^{2}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)\log^{2}T\bigg)\Bigg\}.

These taken collectively with (
163
) allow one to demonstrate that

\max\Bigg\{\frac{f_{0}(x_{t})}{\big(2\pi\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\big)^{-d/2}},\frac{\big(2\pi\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\big)^{-d/2}}{f_{0}(x_{t})}\Bigg\}=\exp\Bigg\{O\bigg(d^{2}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)\log^{2}T\bigg)\Bigg\}=1+O\bigg(d^{2}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)\log^{2}T\bigg),

with the proviso that
d^{2}\big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\big)\log^{2}T\lesssim 1
.

Combining this with (
162
) concludes the proof of Lemma
8
, as long as the two claims in (
161
) are valid (to be justified in Appendix
C.1.1
).

C.1.1
Proof of auxiliary claims (
161
) in Lemma
8

Proof of relation (
161a
).

Recall from (
38
) that

\displaystyle J_{t-1}(x)-I

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\mathbb{E}\left[x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}X_{0}\mid X_{t-1}=x\right]\Big(\mathbb{E}\left[x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}X_{0}\mid X_{t-1}=x\right]\Big)^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}X_{0}\big)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}X_{0}\big)^{\top}\mid X_{t-1}=x\right].

Recognizing that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\big[ZZ^{\top}\big]-\mathbb{E}[Z]\mathbb{E}[Z]^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbb{E}\Big[\big(Z-\mathbb{E}[Z]\big)\big(Z-\mathbb{E}[Z]\big)^{\top}\Big]\right\|\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}\big[ZZ^{\top}\big]\right\|\leq\mathbb{E}\big[\left\|ZZ^{\top}\right\|\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[\|Z\|_{2}^{2}\big]

for any random vector
Z
, we can readily obtain

\displaystyle\big\|J_{t-1}(x)-I\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\mid X_{t-1}=x\right].

(165)

When
(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}
,
it follows from Lemma
2
that

-\log p_{X_{t-1}}(x)\leq c_{6}d\log T

(166)

for any
x
lying in the line segment connecting
x_{t-1}
and
\widehat{x}_{t}
.
With this result in place,
taking (
165
) together with the bound (
43b
) in Lemma
1
immediately leads to

\displaystyle\big\|J_{t-1}(x)-I\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\cdot\left\{d\big(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}\big)\log T\right\}\asymp d\log T

for any
x
lying within the line segment between
x_{t-1}
and

x_{t}/\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}
, as claimed.

Proof of relation (
161b
).

To prove this result, we observe that

\displaystyle\left|\left(1-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\right)\frac{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t}(x_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right|\leq\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1+\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\frac{\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\|_{2}\mid X_{t}=x_{t}\big]}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{\log T}{T}\cdot\sqrt{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}\cdot\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T}\lesssim\frac{d\log^{2}T}{T^{3/2}},

where the last line comes from Lemma
1
as well as the basic property (
39a
) about the learning rates.

Proof of relation (
161c
).

To begin with, the triangle inequality together with the fact
\overline{\alpha}_{t}=\prod_{k=1}^{t}\alpha_{k}
gives

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t})(\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0})\mathrm{d}x_{0}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}-\frac{\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})\mathrm{d}x_{0}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\frac{\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})\mathrm{d}x_{0}-\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})\mathrm{d}x_{0}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})\mathrm{d}x_{0}\bigg\|_{2}.

(167)

Let us first consider the last term in (
167
).
According to Lemma
1
, given that
-\log p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\leq\frac{1}{2}c_{6}d\log T
for some constant
c_{6}>0
, one has

\displaystyle\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T}.

(168)

This in turn reveals that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg)\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})\mathrm{d}x_{0}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg|\cdot\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T}

\displaystyle\qquad\asymp\frac{(1-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}})(1+\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}\sqrt{\alpha_{t}})}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T}

\displaystyle\qquad\asymp\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3/2}}\sqrt{d\log T},

(169)

where the last inequality makes use of the properties (
39
).

Next, we turn attention to the first term in (
167
), which relies on the following claim.

Claim 2
.

Consider any point
x_{t}
obeying
-\log p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\leq c_{6}d\log T
for some large constant
c_{6}>0
.
One has

\displaystyle p_{X_{t-1}}(\widehat{x}_{t})

\displaystyle=\bigg(1+O\Big(\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\Big)\bigg)p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}),

(170a)

In addition, by defining the following set

\mathcal{E}_{1}\coloneqq\big\{x:\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x\|_{2}\leq c_{4}\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T}\big\}

for some large enough constant
c_{4}>0
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}(\widehat{x}_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}{p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}

\displaystyle=1+O\bigg(\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\bigg),\qquad

\displaystyle\text{if }x_{0}\in\mathcal{E}_{1},

(170b)

\displaystyle\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}(\widehat{x}_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}{p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\Bigg(\frac{16c_{1}\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\log T}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})T}\Bigg),\qquad

\displaystyle\text{if }x_{0}\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}.

(170c)

As an immediate consequence of this claim, one has

\displaystyle\frac{p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t})}{p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})}

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}(\widehat{x}_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}{p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}\cdot\frac{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}{p_{X_{t-1}}(\widehat{x}_{t})}=\left(1+O\bigg(\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\bigg)\right)\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}(\widehat{x}_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}{p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}

\displaystyle\begin{cases}=1+O\Big(\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\Big),&\text{if }x_{0}\in\mathcal{E}_{1},\\
\leq\exp\Big(\frac{16c_{1}\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\log T}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})T}\Big),\quad&\text{if }x_{0}\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}.\end{cases}

(171)

In turn, this allows one to deduce that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})\mathrm{d}x_{0}-\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})\mathrm{d}x_{0}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{x_{0}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}}\big|p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t})-p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})\big|\cdot\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{x_{0}\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}\big|p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t})-p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})\big|\cdot\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{x_{0}}O\bigg(\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\bigg)p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{x_{0}\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}\exp\Bigg(\frac{16c_{1}\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\log T}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})T}\Bigg)p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle\quad\leq O\bigg(\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\bigg)\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}\mid X_{t}=x_{0}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{x_{0}\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}\exp\Bigg(\frac{20c_{1}\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\log T}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})T}\Bigg)p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})\mathrm{d}x_{0}.

Invoking (
43a
) in Lemma
1
as well as
similar arugments for (
118
) (assuming that
c_{4}

is sufficiently large), we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}\bigg\|\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})\mathrm{d}x_{0}-\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0})\mathrm{d}x_{0}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\lesssim\frac{d^{3/2}(1-\alpha_{t})\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\log^{3/2}T}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})^{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}\cdot\frac{1}{T^{c_{0}}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\asymp\frac{d^{3/2}(1-\alpha_{t})\log^{3/2}T}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})^{3/2}},

(172)

where
c_{0}
is some large enough constant, and we have also made use of the properties in (
39
).

Substituting (
169
) and (
172
) into (
167
) readily concludes the proof.

Proof of Claim
2
.

Let us make the following observations: for any
x_{0}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}(\widehat{x}_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}{p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\bigg)^{d/2}\exp\bigg(\frac{\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{\big\|\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\exp\Bigg\{\big(1+o(1)\big)\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}(1-\alpha_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\cdot\frac{d}{2}\Bigg\}\exp\bigg(-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\exp\Bigg\{O\bigg(\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\bigg)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\exp\Bigg\{O\bigg(\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}+\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\bigg)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=1+O\bigg(\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\bigg),

(173)

where the second line holds since

\log\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}=\log\bigg(1+\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}(1-\alpha_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\bigg)=\big(1+o(1)\big)\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}(1-\alpha_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}},

and we have also made use of (
39
) (so that
\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}=o(1)
under our assumption on
T
).
Additionally, for any
x_{0}\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}
, it follows from the argument for (
110
) that

\displaystyle\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}(\widehat{x}_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}{p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{0}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\Bigg(\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\Bigg(\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2c_{4}^{2}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})^{2}\log T}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\Bigg(\frac{8c_{1}\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\log T}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})T}\Bigg)\leq\exp\Bigg(\frac{16c_{1}\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\log T}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})T}\Bigg),

where the last line comes from (
39b
) and (
39c
).
Taking the above bounds together and invoking the same calculation as in (
116
), (
118
) and (
121
), we reach

\displaystyle p_{X_{t-1}}(\widehat{x}_{t})

\displaystyle=\bigg(1+O\Big(\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\Big)\bigg)p_{X_{t}}(x_{t}),

thus concluding the proof of Claim
2
.
∎

C.2
Proof of Lemma
9

First, recognizing that
X_{t}
follows a Gaussian distribution when conditioned on
X_{t-1}=x_{t-1}
, we can derive

\displaystyle\log p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})

\displaystyle=\log\frac{p_{X_{t}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{t}\,|\,x_{t-1})p_{X_{t-1}}(x_{t-1})}{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}

\displaystyle=\log\frac{p_{X_{t-1}}(x_{t-1})}{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}+\frac{\|x_{t}-\sqrt{{\alpha_{t}}}x_{t-1}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}-\frac{d}{2}\log\big(2\pi(1-\alpha_{t})\big)

\displaystyle\leq\log\frac{p_{X_{t-1}}(x_{t-1})}{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}+T\big(\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+1\big),

where the last inequality makes use of the properties (
39
) about
\alpha_{t}

(recall that
\widehat{x}_{t}
has been defined in (
77
)).
Some direct calculations then yield

\displaystyle\log\frac{p_{X_{t-1}}(x_{t-1})}{p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})}

\displaystyle=\log\frac{\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}}(x_{0})\exp\Big(-\frac{\|x_{t-1}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}\Big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}}{\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}}(x_{0})\exp\Big(-\frac{\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\Big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}}-\frac{d}{2}\log\Big(\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{x_{0}:\|x_{0}\|_{2}\leq T^{c_{R}}}\Bigg\{\frac{\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{\|x_{t-1}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}\Bigg\}+\frac{d}{2}\log 2

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{x_{0}:\|x_{0}\|_{2}\leq T^{c_{R}}}\frac{\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}+\frac{d}{2}\log 2

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{x_{0}:\|x_{0}\|_{2}\leq T^{c_{R}}}\frac{\|x_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\|x_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}+\frac{d}{2}\log 2

\displaystyle\leq 2T\left(\|x_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+T^{2c_{R}}\right),

where the second line makes use of the properties in (
39
).
Combining the above two relations, we arrive at

\displaystyle\log p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})

\displaystyle\leq 2T\big(\|x_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+T^{2c_{R}}\big).

(174)

We then turn attention to the conditional distribution
p_{Y^{\star}_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t}}
.
First, it follows from (
76b
) that

\displaystyle\log\frac{1}{p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{t}\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}+\frac{d}{2}\log\bigg(2\pi\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\bigg)

(175)

\displaystyle\leq T\left(\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\|x_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+T^{2c_{R}}\right)+d\log T,

(176)

with
\mu_{t}(x_{t})
defined in (
74b
).
To see why the last inequality follows, we recall from (
74b
) that

\displaystyle\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+2\|\widehat{x}_{t}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=2\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+2\bigg(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\bigg)^{2}\Big\|\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t})\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}\Big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{2(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{\alpha_{t}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})^{2}}\sup_{x_{0}:\|x_{0}\|_{2}\leq T^{c_{R}}}\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{64c_{1}^{2}\log^{2}T}{T^{2}}\bigg(2\|x_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+2\overline{\alpha}_{t}T^{2c_{R}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq 2\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\|x_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+T^{2c_{R}},

(177)

where we have invoked the properties (
39
).

Putting everything together, we arrive at the advertised crude bound:

\log\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}{p_{Y^{\star}_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}\leq 2T\left(\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+\|x_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+T^{2c_{R}}\right).

C.3
Proof of Lemma
10

To begin with, we make the observation that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim q_{t}}\Big[\mathsf{KL}\Big(p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(\cdot\,|\,x_{t})\parallel p_{Y_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t}}(\cdot\,|\,x_{t})\Big)\Big]-\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim q_{t}}\Big[\mathsf{KL}\Big(p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(\cdot\,|\,x_{t})\parallel p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(\cdot\,|\,x_{t})\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle=\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\log\frac{p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}{p_{Y_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle=\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\Big(\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}-\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\Big)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle=\underset{\eqqcolon\,\mathcal{H}_{t}}{\underbrace{\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\bigg(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}(x_{t})^{2}-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\big(s_{t}(x)-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)\bigg)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}}},

(178)

where we have used (
76a
) and (
76b
) as well as the definition (
48
) of
\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}(x)
.
Everything comes down to proving that

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{t}\lesssim\exp\Big(-\frac{\min\{c_{3},c_{6}\}}{8}d\log T\Big)+\frac{d\log^{3}T}{T}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\big[\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}(X_{t})^{2}\big].

(179)

Towards this end, one first notes that the first component of
\mathcal{H}_{t}
obeys

\displaystyle\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}(x_{t})^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\big[\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}(X_{t})^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\log T}{T}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\big[\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}(X_{t})^{2}\big],

(180)

where the last inequality comes from (
39
).
We then switch attention to the second component of
\mathcal{H}_{t}
.
In view of the distribution of
Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}
in (
76b
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\big(s_{t}(x_{t})-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle=\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\big(p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})-p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big)\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\big(s_{t}(x_{t})-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{x_{t}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\left\{\int_{x_{t-1}}p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}\big(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t}\big)\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\right\}\big(s_{t}(x_{t})-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(\text{i})}}{{=}}\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\bigg(1-\frac{p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}\bigg)\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\big(s_{t}(x_{t})-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\underset{\eqqcolon\,\mathcal{K}_{1}}{\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{E}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\bigg(1-\frac{p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}\bigg)\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\big(s_{t}(x_{t})-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underset{\eqqcolon\,\mathcal{K}_{2}}{\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{c}}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\left\{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})+p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\right\}\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}\big\|s_{t}(x_{t})-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}}},

(181)

where
(\text{i})
holds since
\mathbb{E}\big[Y_{t-1}^{\star}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(Y_{t})\mid Y_{t}\big]=0
.
This leaves us with two terms to cope with.

•

Regarding the term
\mathcal{K}_{1}
, we can bound

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{1}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\lesssim}\frac{d^{2}\log^{3}T}{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}(x_{t})\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\lesssim}\frac{d^{3}\log^{3}T}{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}+\frac{d\log^{3}T}{T}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\big[\varepsilon^{2}_{\mathsf{score},t}(X_{t})\big]

\displaystyle=\frac{d^{3}\log^{3}T}{T}\int_{\mathcal{E}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}{p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{d\log^{3}T}{T}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\big[\varepsilon^{2}_{\mathsf{score},t}(X_{t})\big]

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(iii)}}{\lesssim}\frac{d^{3}\log^{3}T}{T}\bigg(1+O\Big(\frac{d^{2}\log^{3}T}{T}\Big)\bigg)\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})}{\alpha_{t}}+\frac{d\log^{3}T}{T}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\big[\varepsilon^{2}_{\mathsf{score},t}(X_{t})\big]+\exp\Big(-\frac{c_{3}}{4}d\log T\Big)

\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(iv)}}{\lesssim}\frac{d^{4}\log^{4}T}{T^{2}}+\frac{d\log^{3}T}{T}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\big[\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}(X_{t})^{2}\big].

(182)

Here, (i) comes from (
84
),
(ii) is due to the elementary inequality
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
,
(iii) invokes the relation (
84
) as well as the Gaussian distribution of
Y_{t-1}^{\star}\mid Y_{t}
in (
76b
),
whereas (iv) applies (
39
).

•

With regards to the term
\mathcal{K}_{2}
,
it follows from the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality that

\mathcal{K}_{2}\lesssim\sqrt{\mathcal{K}_{3}\mathcal{K}_{4}},

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{3}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\int_{\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{c}}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\left\{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})+p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\right\}\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t};

(183a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{4}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\int_{\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{c}}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\left\{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})+p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\right\}\big\|s_{t}(x_{t})-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq 2\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\big\|s_{t}(x_{t})-s_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t}=2\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\left[\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}(X_{t})^{2}\right].

(183b)

Consequently, it suffices to look at the term
\mathcal{K}_{3}
.
According to (
88
), we have

\displaystyle\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg\|x_{t-1}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}x_{t}\bigg\|_{2}^{2}+\left(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right)^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}\mid X_{t}=x_{t}\Big]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg\|x_{t-1}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}x_{t}\bigg\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\log^{2}T}{T^{2}}\left(\|x_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+T^{2c_{R}}\right),

where the last inequality makes use of (
39
)
and the assumption (
21
).
Combining this with (
87
) gives

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{c}}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\int_{\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{c}}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\bigg\|x_{t-1}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}x_{t}\bigg\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{\log^{2}T}{T^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{c}}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\left(\|x_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+T^{2c_{R}}\right)\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\exp\left(-c_{3}d\log T\right).

Additionally, let us decompose

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{c}}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\underset{\eqqcolon\,\mathcal{K}_{5}}{\underbrace{\int_{(x_{t},x_{t-1}):p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})<e^{-0.5c_{6}d\log T}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underset{\eqqcolon\,\mathcal{K}_{6}}{\underbrace{\int_{(x_{t},x_{t-1}):p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\geq e^{-0.5c_{6}d\log T},\|x_{t}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}>c_{3}\sqrt{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})p_{Y_{t-1}^{\star}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{t-1}\mathrm{d}x_{t}}}.

Substitution into (
183a
) yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\mathcal{K}_{3}\mathcal{K}_{4}}\lesssim\sqrt{\big\{\exp\left(-c_{3}d\log T\right)+\mathcal{K}_{5}+\mathcal{K}_{6}\big\}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\left[\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}(X_{t})^{2}\right]},

(184)

thus motivating us to bound
\mathcal{K}_{5}
and
\mathcal{K}_{6}
separately.

–

Regarding
\mathcal{K}_{6}
, we can use (
89
) to demonstrate that

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{6}

\displaystyle\leq\int p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|Y_{t-1}^{\star}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(Y_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathds{1}\Big\{\big\|Y_{t-1}^{\star}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(Y_{t})\big\|_{2}\geq\frac{c_{3}}{2}\sqrt{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}\Big\}\mid Y_{t}=x_{t}\right]\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\left(-\frac{c_{3}}{4}d\log T\right).

–

Turning to
\mathcal{K}_{5}
, one can invoke (
62
) to derive

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{x_{t}:p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})<e^{-0.5c_{6}d\log T}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|Y_{t-1}^{\star}-\mu_{t}^{\star}(Y_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\mid Y_{t}=x_{t}\right]\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq d\int_{x_{t}:p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})<e^{-0.5c_{6}d\log T}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\mathrm{d}x_{t}

\displaystyle\leq d\int_{x_{t}:p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})<e^{-0.5c_{6}d\log T},\|x_{t}\|_{2}\leq T^{c_{R}+2}}p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\mathrm{d}x_{t}+\mathbb{P}\left\{\|X_{t}\|_{2}\geq T^{c_{R}+2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq d\exp\Big(-\frac{1}{2}c_{6}d\log T\Big)\cdot(2T^{c_{R}+2})^{d}+\exp(-c_{6}d\log T)

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\Big(-\frac{1}{4}c_{6}d\log T\Big),

provided that
c_{6}>0
is large enough.

Combining the above results with (
184
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\Big\{\exp\left(-\frac{c_{3}}{4}d\log T\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{c_{6}}{4}d\log T\right)\Big\}\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\left[\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}(X_{t})^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\Big(-\frac{\min\{c_{3},c_{6}\}}{4}d\log T\Big)+\exp\Big(-\frac{\min\{c_{3},c_{6}\}}{4}d\log T\Big)\mathbb{E}_{X_{t}\sim q_{t}}\left[\varepsilon_{\mathsf{score},t}(X_{t})^{2}\right].

(185)

Taking the above bounds (
180
), (
182
) and (
185
) together with (
178
) immediately finishes the proof.

Appendix D
Analysis for the accelerated deterministic sampler (Theorem
2
)

The aim of this section is to establish Theorem
2
.
The proof follows similar arguments as that of Theorem
1
.

D.1
Proof of Theorem
2

Auxiliary vectors and their properties.

Before embarking on the proof, let us introduce several pieces of notation:

\displaystyle\varphi_{t}(x)

\displaystyle\coloneqq x-u_{t}(x),

(186a)

where

\displaystyle u_{t}(x)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\{\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\|g_{t}(x)\|_{2}^{2}\bigg\}g_{t}(x)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\mathbb{E}\left[\big(X_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big)\big(X_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big)^{\top}\,|\,X_{t}=x\right]g_{t}(x)

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|X_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big(X_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}-g_{t}(X_{t})\big)\,|\,X_{t}=x\right]

(186b)

\displaystyle=-\bigg\{\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8}\|s_{t}^{\star}(x)\|_{2}^{2}\bigg\}s_{t}^{\star}(x)-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\mathbb{E}\left[\overline{W}_{t}\overline{W}_{t}^{\top}s_{t}^{\star}(X_{t})\,|\,X_{t}=x\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|\overline{W}_{t}\big\|_{2}^{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\overline{W}_{t}+(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})s_{t}^{\star}(X_{t})\bigg)\,|\,X_{t}=x\right].

(186c)

Here, we recall that
g_{t}(x)=-(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})s_{t}^{\star}(x)
has been defined in (
36
)
and that
X_{t}=\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}+\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\,\overline{W}_{t}
with
\overline{W}_{t}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,I_{d})
(see (
6
)).
In view of Assumption
3
and the fact that the MMSE estimator is the conditional expectation
(
Hajek, 2015
, Section 3.3.1)
,
we have

\displaystyle w_{t}(x)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|\overline{W}_{t}\big\|_{2}^{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\overline{W}_{t}+(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})s_{t}^{\star}(X_{t})\bigg)+\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\overline{W}_{t}\overline{W}_{t}^{\top}s_{t}^{\star}(X_{t})\mid X_{t}=x\right],

which taken together with (
186
) and (
27
) confirms the following equivalent expression for the sampler (
27
):

Y_{t-1}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\varphi_{t}\big(Y_{t}\big)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}Y_{t}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}u_{t}(Y_{t}).

(187)

We now single out a useful property about
u_{t}(\cdot)
. For any point
x_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
obeying
-\log p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\leq c_{6}d\log T
for some large constant
c_{6}>0
(see Lemma
1
), one has

\displaystyle\big\|u_{t}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}\lesssim(1-\alpha_{t})\Big(\frac{d\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{1/2},

(188a)

and one can also write

\displaystyle u_{t}(x_{t})

\displaystyle=\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}g_{t}(x_{t})+\xi_{t}(x_{t})

(188b)

for some residual term
\xi_{t}(x_{t})
obeying

\displaystyle\big\|\xi_{t}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}\bigg(\frac{d\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg)^{3/2}.

(188c)

To streamline presentation, we leave the proof of (
188
) to the end of this subsection.

Main steps of the proof.

Akin to the proof of Theorem
1
, the key idea lies in understanding the transformation
\Phi_{t}
(cf. (
27
)), or equivalently,
\varphi_{t}
(cf. (
186
)).
There are several objects that play an important role in the analysis, which we single out as follows:

\displaystyle A_{t}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0};

(189a)

\displaystyle B_{t}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg\|\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}\bigg\|_{2}^{2};

(189b)

\displaystyle C_{t}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{1}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{4}\mathrm{d}x_{0};

(189c)

\displaystyle D_{t}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{1}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\Big(\big\langle g_{t}(x),\,x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\rangle\Big)^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0};

(189d)

\displaystyle E_{t}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{1}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big\langle g_{t}(x),\,x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\rangle\mathrm{d}x_{0}.

(189e)

Here, we suppress the dependency on
x
in the above five objects to simplify notation whenever it is clear from the context. In view of Lemma
1
and the properties (
39
),
we have the following bounds:

\displaystyle\big|B_{t}\big|\leq\big|A_{t}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\cdot d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T\asymp d\log T;

(190a)

\displaystyle\big|C_{t}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}d^{2}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}\log^{2}T\asymp d^{2}\log^{2}T;

(190b)

\displaystyle\big|D_{t}\big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|g_{t}(x)\|_{2}^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}\lesssim d^{2}\log^{2}T;

(190c)

\displaystyle\big|E_{t}\big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|g_{t}(x)\|_{2}^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{3}\mathrm{d}x_{0}\lesssim d^{2}\log^{2}T.

(190d)

As it turns out, Theorem
2
can be established in a very similar way as in the proof of Theorem
1
.
In essence, the only step that needs to be changed is to replace (
58
) in Lemma
4
with (
191
) in the lemma below.

Lemma 11
.

Suppose that
\frac{d^{2}(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\lesssim 1
. For every
x\in\real
obeying
-\log p_{X_{t}}(x)\leq c_{6}d\log T
for some large enough constant
c_{6}>0
,
we have

\displaystyle\frac{p_{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}X_{t-1}}(\varphi_{t}(x))}{p_{X_{t}}(x)}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})(d+B_{t}-A_{t})}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}+O\Big(d^{3}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{3}\log^{3}T\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\big[d(d+2)+(4+2d)(B_{t}-A_{t})-B_{t}^{2}+C_{t}+2D_{t}-3E_{t}+A_{t}B_{t}\big].

(191a)

Moreover, for any random vector
Y
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{p_{\varphi_{t}(Y)}(\varphi_{t}(x))}{p_{Y}(x)}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})(d+B_{t}-A_{t})}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}+O\Big(d^{6}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{3}\log^{3}T\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\big[d(d+2)+(4+2d)(B_{t}-A_{t})-B_{t}^{2}+C_{t}+2D_{t}-3E_{t}+A_{t}B_{t}\big].

(191b)

Here, the quantities
A_{t},\ldots,E_{t}
are defined in (
189
).

The proof of this lemma can be found in Appendix
D.2
.
Crucially, the terms in (
191
) and those in (
191
) coincide (except for the residual terms

O\big(d^{3}\big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\big)^{3}\log^{3}T\big)
and
O\big(d^{6}\big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\big)^{3}\log^{3}T\big)
),
which will cancel each other during the subsequent proof.

With Lemma
11
in place, we claim that for every
t\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big(Y_{t}\in{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\big)\geq 1-(T-t+1)\exp(-c_{3}d\log T)

(192)

for some constant
c_{3}>0
, where the set
\mathcal{E}_{t}
is defined as

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\coloneqq\bigg\{y:\Big|\frac{p_{Y_{t}}(y)}{p_{X_{t}}(y)}-1\Big|\leq c_{5}(T-t+1)\frac{d^{6}\log^{6}T}{T^{3}}\bigg\}

for some constant
c_{5}>0
. We leave its proof to the end of this section.

Suppose for the moment that the above claim (
192
) is valid.
Then taking
t=1
leads to

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(Y_{1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1})=\mathbb{P}\Bigg(\bigg|\frac{q_{1}(Y_{1})}{p_{1}(Y_{1})}-1\bigg|\leq\frac{c_{5}d^{6}\log^{6}T}{T^{2}}\Bigg)\geq 1-T\exp\big(-c_{3}d\log T\big),

which also reveals that

\int_{y\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}p_{1}(y)\mathrm{d}y\leq T\exp\big(-c_{3}d\log T\big).

Additionally, we make the observation that

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\int}_{y\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}\big|p_{1}(y)-q_{1}(y)\big|\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle\leq{\displaystyle\int}_{y\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}p_{1}(y)\mathrm{d}y+{\displaystyle\int}_{y\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}q_{1}(y)\mathrm{d}y={\displaystyle\int}_{y\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}p_{1}(y)\mathrm{d}y+1-{\displaystyle\int}_{y\in\mathcal{E}_{1}}q_{1}(y)\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle\leq{\displaystyle\int}_{y\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}p_{1}(y)\mathrm{d}y+1-{\displaystyle\int}_{y\in\mathcal{E}_{1}}p_{1}(y)\mathrm{d}y+{\displaystyle\int}_{y\in\mathcal{E}_{1}}\big|p_{1}(y)-q_{1}(y)\big|\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=2{\displaystyle\int}_{y\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}p_{1}(y)\mathrm{d}y+{\displaystyle\int}_{y\in\mathcal{E}_{1}}\big|p_{1}(y)-q_{1}(y)\big|\mathrm{d}y.

The above results together with the definition of the total variation distance gives

\displaystyle\mathsf{TV}\big(q_{1},p_{1}\big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{y\in\mathcal{E}_{1}}\big|q_{1}(y)-p_{1}(y)\big|\mathrm{d}y+\frac{1}{2}\int_{y\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}\big|q_{1}(y)-p_{1}(y)\big|\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle\leq\int_{y\in\mathcal{E}_{1}}\big|q_{1}(y)-p_{1}(y)\big|\mathrm{d}y+{\displaystyle\int}_{y\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}p_{1}(y)\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{Y_{1}\sim p_{1}}\bigg[\Big|\frac{q_{1}(Y_{1})}{p_{1}(Y_{1})}-1\Big|\cdot\ind\left\{Y_{1}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}\right\}\bigg]+{\displaystyle\int}_{y\notin\mathcal{E}_{1}}p_{1}(y)\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{5}d^{6}\log^{6}T}{T^{2}}+\exp\big(-c_{3}d\log T\big)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{d^{6}\log^{6}T}{T^{2}}.

(193)

This establishes the advertised result in Theorem
2
,
provided that Claim (
192
) can be verified.

Proof of properties (
188
).

To justify the above results (
188
), note that Lemma
1
implies that

\displaystyle\big\|g_{t}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|X_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}\,|\,X_{t}=x_{t}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T},

(194a)

\displaystyle\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\big\|g_{t}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}\lesssim\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T}

\displaystyle\asymp(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}\sqrt{\frac{d\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}},

(194b)

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\big\|g_{t}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}\lesssim\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T}

\displaystyle\asymp(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}\frac{\sqrt{d\log T}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3/2}},

(194c)

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\|g_{t}(x_{t})\|_{2}^{3}\lesssim\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\left(d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T\right)^{3/2}

\displaystyle\asymp(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}\bigg(\frac{d\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg)^{3/2},

(194d)

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big)\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big)^{\top}\,|\,X_{t}=x_{t}\right]g_{t}(x_{t})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}\big\|g_{t}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,X_{t}=x_{t}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}\bigg(\frac{d\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg)^{3/2},

(194e)

and

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}-g_{t}(X_{t})\big)\,|\,X_{t}=x_{t}\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\left\{\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}^{3}\,|\,X_{t}=x_{t}\right]\right\|_{2}+\big\|g_{t}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\,|\,X_{t}=x_{t}\right]\right\|_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}\bigg(\frac{d\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg)^{3/2}.

(194f)

Substituting the bounds (
194
) into (
186b
) immediately establishes (
188
).
∎

Proof of the claim (
192
).

We would like to prove this claim by induction,
for which we start with the base case with
t=T
.
Recall that
X_{T}\overset{\mathrm{d}}{=}\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{T}}X_{0}+\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{T}}B
and
Y_{T}\overset{\mathrm{d}}{=}B

with
B\sim\mathcal{N}(0,I_{d})
independent of
X_{0}
,
and that
\|X_{0}\|_{2}\leq R
with
R=T^{c_{R}}
for some constant
c_{R}>0
.
For large enough
T
, it immediately follows from (
39d
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(Y_{T}\in{\mathcal{E}}_{t})\geq 1-\exp(-c_{3}d\log T)

(195)

for some constant
c_{3}>0
large enough.

Suppose now that the claim (
192
) holds for some
t\geq 2
, and we wish to prove the claim for
t-1.

We would first like to claim that with probability at least
1-(T-t)\exp(-c_{3}d\log T)
for some constant
c_{3}>0
,
one has

\displaystyle q_{t}(Y_{t})\geq\exp\big(-c_{6}d\log T\big).

(196)

With (
196
) in place, one sees that Lemma
11
is applicable to
x=Y_{t}
with high probability.

Next, consider any
y
obeying
-\log p_{X_{t}}(y)\leq c_{6}d\log T
.
With these bounds (
190
) in mind, applying relations (
191
) and (
191
) in Lemma
11
leads to

\displaystyle\frac{p_{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}Y_{t-1}}\big(\phi_{t}(y)\big)}{p_{Y_{t}}(y)}\bigg(\frac{p_{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}X_{t-1}}\big(\phi_{t}(y)\big)}{p_{X_{t}}(y)}\bigg)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{\phi_{t}(Y_{t})}\big(\phi_{t}(y)\big)}{p_{Y_{t}}(y)}\bigg(\frac{p_{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}X_{t-1}}\big(\phi_{t}(y)\big)}{p_{X_{t}}(y)}\bigg)^{-1}

\displaystyle=1+O\Bigg(d^{6}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{3}\log^{3}T\Bigg).

Replacing
y
with
Y_{t}
in the above display, using the fact that
Y_{t-1}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\phi_{t}(Y_{t})
, and invoking the relation (
55
),
we immediately arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{p_{t-1}(Y_{t-1})}{q_{t-1}(Y_{t-1})}

\displaystyle=\left\{1+O\Bigg(d^{6}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{3}\log^{3}T\Bigg)\right\}\cdot\frac{p_{t}(Y_{t})}{q_{t}(Y_{t})}

with probability exceeding
1-(T-t)\exp(-c_{3}d\log T)
. This concludes the proof of Claim (
192
) via standard induction arguments.
∎

D.2
Proof of Lemma
11

The proof of Lemma
11
is derived in a very similar way to the proof of Lemma
4
,
as detailed below.
For notational simplicity, we shall abbreviate

\displaystyle u=u_{t}(x)\qquad\text{and}\qquad z=g_{t}(x)

(197)

whenever it is clear from the context.

D.2.1
Proof of relation (
191
)

Through direct calculations as shown in (
127
), we can obtain

\displaystyle p_{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}X_{t-1}}\big(\varphi_{t}(x)\big)=p_{X_{t}}(x)\bigg(\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg)^{d/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\cdot\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\exp\bigg(-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{\|u\|_{2}^{2}-2u^{\top}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\bigg)\mathrm{d}x_{0}.

(198)

Moreover, similar to the analysis of Lemma
4
, we focus our attention on the set given
x
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}\coloneqq\big\{x_{0}:\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}\leq 5c_{5}\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T}\big\},

(199)

which allows us to derive, for some numerical constant
c_{8}>0
,

\displaystyle\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\exp\Big(-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{\|u\|_{2}^{2}-2u^{\top}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\Big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle=O\left(\exp(-c_{8}c_{5}^{2}d\log T)\right)+\int_{x_{0}\in\mathcal{E}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\exp\bigg(-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{\|u\|_{2}^{2}-2u^{\top}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\bigg)\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle\eqqcolon\text{RHS}

(200)

To further control the right-hand side above, recall that the learning rates are selected such that
\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\leq\frac{4c_{1}\log T}{T}
for
1<t\leq T
(see (
39b
)). In view of the Taylor expansion

e^{-x}=1-x+\frac{1}{2}x^{2}+O(x^{3})
for
x\leq 1/2
, we can derive

\displaystyle\text{RHS}=O\left(\exp(-c_{8}c_{5}^{2}d\log T)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{x_{0}\in\mathcal{E}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\bigg\{1-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}-\frac{\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\|z\|_{2}^{2}-2u^{\top}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\Big(\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}-z^{\top}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\Big)^{2}+O\Big(d^{3}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{3}\log^{3}T\Big)\bigg\}\mathrm{d}x_{0}.

(201)

In order to see this, we recall the property of
u
(cf. (
188
)) as

\displaystyle\left\|u-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}z\right\|_{2}\leq O\bigg((1-\alpha_{t})^{2}\Big(\frac{d\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{3/2}\bigg).

(202)

As a consequence, for any
x_{0}\in\mathcal{E}
we have

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}

\displaystyle=O\Big(d\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)\log T\Big)

and

\displaystyle\frac{\|u\|_{2}^{2}-2u^{\top}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}

\displaystyle=\frac{\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\|z\|_{2}^{2}-2u^{\top}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}+O\bigg(d^{2}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{3}\log^{2}T\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{z^{\top}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}+O\bigg(d^{2}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{2}\log^{2}T\bigg)

\displaystyle=O\bigg(d\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)\log T\bigg),

where we have invoked the properties (
39
).
Taking the above results together and using the following basic properties regarding quantities
A_{t},\ldots,E_{t}

(defined in (
189
))

\displaystyle\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}=(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})A_{t},

\displaystyle\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\|z\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}=(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})B_{t},

\displaystyle\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)u^{\top}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}=\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2}B_{t}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\big[B_{t}-B_{t}^{2}+D_{t}-E_{t}+A_{t}B_{t}\big],

\displaystyle\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\Big(\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}-z^{\top}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\Big)^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}=(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}\big[C_{t}+D_{t}-2E_{t}\big],

we arrive at

(
D.2.1
)

\displaystyle=1-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})(A_{t}-B_{t})}{2(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\big[-B_{t}^{2}+C_{t}+2D_{t}-3E_{t}+A_{t}B_{t}\big]+O\bigg(d^{3}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{3}\log^{3}T\bigg).

Once again, we note that integrating over set
\mathcal{E}
and over all possible
x_{0}
only incurs a difference at most as large as
O\big(\exp(-c_{8}c_{5}^{2}d\log T)\big)
.
Putting all this together establishes the advertised result (
191
).

D.2.2
Proof of relation (
191
)

Consider any random vector
Y
, and let us invoke again the basic transformation

\displaystyle p_{\varphi_{t}(Y)}(\varphi_{t}(x))

\displaystyle=\mathsf{det}\Big(\frac{\partial\varphi_{t}(x)}{\partial x}\Big)^{-1}p_{Y}(x),

where
\frac{\partial\varphi_{t}(x)}{\partial x}
denotes the Jacobian matrix.
We are then left with controlling the quantity
\mathsf{det}\Big(\frac{\partial\varphi_{t}(x)}{\partial x}\Big)^{-1}
.

Towards this, let us again recall that the determinant of a matrix satisfies

\displaystyle\mathsf{det}(I+A)^{-1}=1-\mathsf{Tr}(A)+\frac{1}{2}\big[\mathsf{Tr}(A)^{2}+\|A\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\big]+O\big(d^{3}\|A\|^{3}\big),

provided that
d\|A\|\leq c_{20}
for some small enough constant
c_{20}>0
.
This relation leads to

\displaystyle p_{\varphi_{t}(Y)}(\varphi_{t}(x))

\displaystyle=\mathsf{det}\Big(\frac{\partial\varphi_{t}(x)}{\partial x}\Big)^{-1}p_{Y}(x)

\displaystyle=\bigg\{1+\mathsf{Tr}\Big(\frac{\partial u}{\partial x}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}\Big[\mathsf{Tr}\Big(\frac{\partial u}{\partial x}\Big)^{2}+\Big\|\frac{\partial u}{\partial x}\Big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\Big]+O\Big(d^{3}\Big\|\frac{\partial u}{\partial x}\Big\|\Big)\bigg\}p_{Y}(x),

(203)

where we invoke the definition in (
186
) that

\displaystyle\varphi_{t}(x)=x-u

\displaystyle=x-\Big(\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\|z\|_{2}^{2}\Big)z

\displaystyle-\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}z\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}-z\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}.

To further control the right-hand side above of the above display, let us first make note of several identities.
Proving these identities only requires elementary calculation regarding Gaussian integration and derivatives, which is omitted here for brevity.
Specifically, one has

\displaystyle\frac{\partial z}{\partial x}

\displaystyle=J_{t},

(204a)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\|z\|_{2}^{2}z

\displaystyle=\|z\|_{2}^{2}J_{t}+2zz^{\top}J_{t},

(204b)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle=\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}I+2\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big(\Big(\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\Big)\Big(\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}\Big)^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad-\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}\mathrm{d}x_{0}\Big),

(204c)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}z=\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}J_{t}+2zz^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big(\Big(\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\Big)zz^{\top}-z\Big(\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big\|x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}\Big)^{\top}\Big),

(204d)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}z\mathrm{d}x_{0}=\|z\|_{2}^{2}I+zz^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}J_{t}\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big(z^{\top}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\big)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)z^{\top}\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big(z^{\top}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\big)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}\mathrm{d}x_{0}.

(204e)

Equipped with the above relations, we can easily verify that

\displaystyle\Big\|\frac{\partial u}{\partial x}\Big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}},

(205a)

\displaystyle\mathsf{Tr}\Big(\frac{\partial u}{\partial x}\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})\big(d+B_{t}-A_{t}\big)}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\big(d-2A_{t}-A_{t}^{2}+3A_{t}B_{t}+2B_{t}-3B_{t}^{2}+C_{t}+4D_{t}-3E_{t}-F_{t}\big),

(205b)

\displaystyle\Big\|\frac{\partial u}{\partial x}\Big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\Big\|\frac{\partial z}{\partial x}\Big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+O\Big(d^{5}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{3}\log^{3}T\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{4(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\big(d+2(B_{t}-A_{t})+B_{t}^{2}+F_{t}-2D_{t}\big)+O\Big(d^{5}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{3}\log^{3}T\Big),

(205c)

as long as
d^{2}\big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\big)\log T\lesssim 1
,
where we recall the definition of the quantities
A_{t}
to
E_{t}
in (
189
), and

\displaystyle F_{t}(x)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\Big\|\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\int_{x_{0}}p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}\mathrm{d}x_{0}\Big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

(205d)

Plugging these results into inequality (
D.2.2
) leads to

\displaystyle p_{\varphi_{t}(Y)}(\varphi_{t}(x))

\displaystyle=p_{Y}(x)\bigg\{1+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})(d+B_{t}-A_{t})}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}+O\Big(d^{6}\Big(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\Big)^{3}\log^{3}T\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}}{8(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\big[d(d+2)+(4+2d)(B_{t}-A_{t})-B_{t}^{2}+C_{t}+2D_{t}-3E_{t}+A_{t}B_{t}\big]\bigg\}.

(205e)

We have thus completed the proof of Lemma
11
.

Appendix E
Analysis for the accelerated stochastic sampler (Theorems
4
)

The proof of Theorem
4
follows similar structure as the proof of Theorem
3
.
Throughout the proof, we shall employ the notation
\widehat{x}_{t}\coloneqq x_{t}/\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}
as before.

E.1
Proof of Theorem
4

Step 1: expressing the update rule in terms of a Jacobian matrix.

Let us recall the Jacobian matrix
J_{t}(x)=\frac{\partial g_{t}(x)}{\partial x}
defined in (
37
).
In view of the expression (
38
) as well as (
6
) (i.e.,
X_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}=\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\,\overline{W}_{t}
with
\overline{W}_{t}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,I_{d})
), one can write

\displaystyle J_{t}(x)

\displaystyle=I_{d}+\mathbb{E}\big[\overline{W}_{t}\mid X_{t}=x\big]\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[\overline{W}_{t}\mid X_{t}=x\big]\Big)^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\big[\overline{W}_{t}\overline{W}_{t}^{\top}\mid X_{t}=x\big]

\displaystyle=I_{d}+\big(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}\big)s_{t}^{\star}(x)s_{t}^{\star}(x)^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\big[\overline{W}_{t}\overline{W}_{t}^{\top}\mid X_{t}=x\big],

(206)

where
the last line makes use of the relation (
36
).
Additionally,
recall that (i)
v_{t}(x,z)
(cf. (
32
)) is the MMSE estimator for estimating
\overline{W}_{t}\overline{W}_{t}z
given
\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}+\sqrt{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\,\overline{W}_{t}=x
and
Z_{t}=z
, and (ii)
Z_{t}
is independent from
X_{0}
and
\overline{W}_{t}
. Then this MMSE estimator admits the following expression
(
Hajek, 2015
, Section 3.3.1)
:

v_{t}(x,z)=\mathbb{E}\big[\overline{W}_{t}\overline{W}_{t}^{\top}z\mid X_{t}=x\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[\overline{W}_{t}\overline{W}_{t}^{\top}\mid X_{t}=x\big]z.

(207)

As a result, the mapping introduced in (
33b
) can be alternatively expressed as:

\displaystyle\Psi_{t}(x,z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\Big(x+(1-\alpha_{t})s_{t}^{\star}(x)\Big)+\sigma_{t}\left\{z-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\left[z+(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})s_{t}^{\star}(x)s_{t}^{\star}(x)^{\top}z-v_{t}(x,z)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\mu_{t}(x)+\sigma_{t}\bigg(I-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x)\bigg)z,

(208)

where we have also used the definition (
74b
) of
\mu_{t}(\cdot)
.
In comparison to the plain DDPM-type sampler (cf. (
30
)),
the key correction term is the second component on the right-hand side of (
208
),
which adjusts the covariance of the additive Gaussian noise.

Equipped with the above expression (
208
),
we can readily express the conditional distribution of
Y_{t-1}
(cf. (
33a
)) given
Y_{t}
such that: for any points
x_{t},x_{t-1}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle p_{Y_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\big(2\pi\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\big)^{d/2}\big|\det\big(I-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\big)\big|}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\exp\Bigg(-\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\bigg\|\Big(I-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\Big)^{-1}\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big)\bigg\|_{2}^{2}\Bigg).

(209)

Step 2: controlling the conditional distributions
p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}
and
p_{Y_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t}}
.

Akin to Step 2 in the proof of Theorem
3
,
we need to look at the conditional distribution
p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}
when restricted to points from the following set:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}\coloneqq\bigg\{(x_{t},x_{t-1})\,|\,-\log p_{X_{t}}(x_{t})\leq\frac{1}{2}c_{6}d\log T,~\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}\leq c_{3}\sqrt{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}\bigg\},

(210)

with the numerical constants
c_{3},c_{6}>0
introduced in Lemma
2
.
The following lemma, which is a counterpart of Lemma
8
for the accelerated sampler, characterizes
p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}
in a fairly tight manner over the set
\mathcal{E}
.
The proof is deferred to Appendix
E.2
.

Lemma 12
.

There exists some large enough numerical constant
c_{\zeta}>0
such that:
for every
(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}
,

\displaystyle p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})=\frac{1}{\big(2\pi\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\big)^{d/2}\big|\det\big(I-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\big)\big|}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\exp\bigg(-\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\bigg\|\bigg(I-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\bigg)^{-1}\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big)\bigg\|_{2}^{2}+\zeta_{t}(x_{t-1},x_{t})\bigg)

(211)

holds for some residual term
\zeta_{t}(x_{t-1},x_{t})
obeying

\big|\zeta_{t}(x_{t-1},x_{t})\big|\leq c_{\zeta}\frac{d^{3}\log^{4.5}T}{T^{3/2}}.

(212)

Here, we recall the definition of
\mu_{t}(\cdot)
(resp.
J_{t}(\cdot)
) in (
74b
) (resp. (
37
)).

Moving beyond the set
\mathcal{E}
, we are still in need of bounding the log density ratio
\log\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}}{p_{Y_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t}}}
for all pairs
(x_{t},x_{t-1})
outside
\mathcal{E}
,
in a way similar to Lemma
9
in the proof of Theorem
3
.
Our crude bound towards this end is stated as follows, whose proof is postponed to Appendix
E.3
.

Lemma 13
.

For all
(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\real^{d}\times\real^{d}
, we have

\displaystyle\log\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}{p_{Y_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}

\displaystyle\leq T^{c_{0}+2c_{R}+2}\left\{\big\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big\|_{2}^{2}+\|x_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+1\right\},

(213)

where
c_{0}
is defined in (
22
).

Step 3: bounding the KL divergence of interest.

With Lemmas
12
-
13
in place,
one can repeat the arguments in Step 3 in the proof of Theorem
3
to arrive at

\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim X_{t}}\Big[\mathsf{KL}\Big(p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(\cdot\,|\,x_{t})\parallel p_{Y_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t}}(\cdot\,|\,x_{t})\Big)\Big]\lesssim\left(\frac{d^{3}\log^{4.5}T}{T^{3/2}}\right)^{2}.

Substitution into (
79
) and (
78
) then yields

2\mathsf{TV}(p_{X_{1}},p_{Y_{1}})^{2}\leq\mathsf{KL}(p_{X_{1}}\parallel p_{Y_{1}})\lesssim\mathsf{KL}(p_{X_{T}}\parallel p_{Y_{T}})+\sum_{t\geq 2}^{T}\frac{d^{6}\log^{9}T}{T^{3}}\asymp\frac{d^{6}\log^{9}T}{T^{2}},

where the last relation results from (
122
). This completes the proof of Theorem
4
.

E.2
Proof of Lemma
12

Recall that an explicit expression for
p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})
has already been established in Lemma
8
(see (
160
))
A little algebra then allows one to write

\displaystyle p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})

\displaystyle=f_{1}(x_{t})\exp\Big(-f_{2}(x_{t},x_{t-1})+\zeta_{t,1}(x_{t},x_{t-1})\Big),

(214)

for some function
f_{1}(\cdot)
, where

\displaystyle g_{t-1}(x)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\int_{x_{0}}\big(x-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big)p_{X_{0}|X_{t-1}}(x_{0}\,|\,x)\mathrm{d}x_{0},

(215a)

\displaystyle f_{2}(x_{t},x_{t-1})

\displaystyle=\frac{\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}x_{t-1}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}+\frac{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}+\frac{\frac{1}{2}(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}J_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}},

(215b)

\displaystyle\zeta_{t,1}(x_{t},x_{t-1})

\displaystyle=(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}\frac{\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\gamma\left[J_{t-1}\Big((1-\tau)\widehat{x}_{t}+\tau x_{t}(\gamma)\Big)-J_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\right]\mathrm{d}\tau\mathrm{d}\gamma}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big),

(215c)

and we remind the readers that
x_{t}(\gamma)=\gamma x_{t-1}+(1-\gamma)\widehat{x}_{t}
with
\widehat{x}_{t}=x_{t}/\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}
.

In order to control (
214
), we single out two useful facts: for every
(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}
(cf. (
210
)),

\displaystyle\big\|J_{t-1}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\big)-J_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\big\|\lesssim d^{2}\sqrt{\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}}\log^{2}T,\qquad\forall\gamma\in[0,1]

(216)

and

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{J_{t-1}(\widehat{x}_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}-\frac{J_{t}(x_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg\|\lesssim\frac{d^{2}(1-\alpha_{t})\log^{2}T}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}.

(217)

The proofs of these two facts are postponed to Appendix
E.2.1
.
These facts in turn allow us to bound

\big|\zeta_{t,1}(x_{t},x_{t-1})\big|\leq\frac{\big\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}\sup_{\gamma\in[0,1]}\big\|J_{t-1}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\big)-J_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\big\|\lesssim d^{3}\bigg(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\bigg)^{3/2}\log^{3}T

(218)

and
