\displaystyle\left|\frac{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}J_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}-\frac{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}J_{t}(x_{t})\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\big\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big\|_{2}^{2}\bigg\|\frac{J_{t-1}(\widehat{x}_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}-\frac{J_{t}(x_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg\|\lesssim\frac{d^{3}(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}\log^{3}T}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}\asymp\frac{d^{3}(1-\alpha_{t})^{2}\log^{3}T}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})^{2}}

(219)

for any
(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}
.
Consequently, there exists some function
f_{3}(\cdot)
such that

\displaystyle p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})

\displaystyle=f_{3}(x_{t})\exp\Big(-f_{4}(x_{t},x_{t-1})+\zeta_{t,2}(x_{t},x_{t-1})\Big),

(220a)

where

\displaystyle f_{4}(x_{t},x_{t-1})

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{t}\|\widehat{x}_{t}-x_{t-1}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}+\frac{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}+\frac{\frac{1}{2}(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}J_{t}(x_{t})\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}},

(220b)

\displaystyle\big|\zeta_{t,2}(x_{t},x_{t-1})\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim d^{3}\bigg(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\bigg)^{3/2}\log^{3}T\lesssim\frac{d^{3}\log^{4.5}T}{T^{3/2}}.

(220c)

To continue, we further observe that

\left|\frac{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}-\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t}(x_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right|\lesssim\frac{d^{3}\log^{3.5}T}{T^{3/2}},

(221)

which is an immediate consequence of the following two bounds (obtained using (
161c
), (
161b
) and (
39b
)):

\displaystyle\left|\frac{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t-1}\big(\widehat{x}_{t}\big)}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}-\frac{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t}(x_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim d^{2}\sqrt{\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})^{3}}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{3}}}\log^{2}T\lesssim\frac{d^{3}\log^{3.5}T}{T^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\left|\left(1-\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}\right)\frac{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t}(x_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d\log^{2}T}{T^{3/2}}.

This bound (
221
) allows us to replace the second term on the right-hand side of (
220b
) with
\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t}(x_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}
.
It is also seen from (
161a
) and the properties (
39
)
that

\left\|\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\right\|\lesssim\frac{\log T}{T}\cdot d\log T\asymp\frac{d\log^{2}T}{T}=o(1),

(222)

and therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\left(1-\alpha_{t}\right)\frac{(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}J_{t}(x_{t})\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d\log^{2}T}{T}\cdot\big\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\frac{\left(1-\alpha_{t}\right)d^{2}\log^{3}T}{T}\lesssim\frac{d^{2}\log^{4}T}{T^{2}}.

These combined with (
220
) allow us to show that: there exist some functions
f_{5}(\cdot)
and
\widetilde{f}_{5}(\cdot)
such that

\displaystyle p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})

\displaystyle=f_{5}(x_{t})\exp\Big(-f_{6}(x_{t},x_{t-1})+\zeta_{t,3}(x_{t},x_{t-1})\Big)

(223a)

where

\displaystyle f_{6}(x_{t},x_{t-1})

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{t}\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}+\frac{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}g_{t}(x_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}+\frac{\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{t}(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}J_{t}(x_{t})\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\left\{\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t})^{\top}J_{t}(x_{t})\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\right\}+\widetilde{f}_{t}(x_{t}),

(223b)

\displaystyle\big|\zeta_{t,3}(x_{t},x_{t-1})\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d^{3}\log^{4.5}T}{T^{3/2}}.

(223c)

To further proceed, we make note of another useful fact:

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha_{t}}{1-\alpha_{t}}\left|\big(x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\bigg(\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}J_{t}(x_{t})\bigg)\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}g_{t}(x_{t})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{\alpha_{t}}{1-\alpha_{t}}\big\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\right\|\cdot\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}\,|\,X_{t}=x_{t}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}\cdot\frac{d\log^{2}T}{T}\cdot\frac{\log T}{T}\cdot\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\log T}

\displaystyle\quad\asymp\frac{d^{2}\log^{4}T}{T^{2}}\sqrt{1-\alpha_{t}}\lesssim\frac{d^{2}\log^{4.5}T}{T^{3/2}},

where we have made use of the crude bound (
222
) in conjunction with the properties (
39
).
Taking this observation together with (
223
) and (
74b
),
we can apply a little algebra to derive

\displaystyle p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})

\displaystyle=f_{7}(x_{t})\exp\Big(-f_{8}(x_{t},x_{t-1})+\zeta_{t,4}(x_{t},x_{t-1})\Big)

(224a)

for some function
f_{7}(\cdot)
, where

\displaystyle f_{8}(x_{t},x_{t-1})

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\left\{\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\bigg(I+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\bigg)\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big)\right\},

(224b)

\displaystyle\big|\zeta_{t,4}(x_{t},x_{t-1})\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d^{3}\log^{4.5}T}{T^{3/2}}.

(224c)

Note, however, that the covariance matrix
I+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})

still differs from the desired one
\big(I-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\big)^{-2}
.
As it turns out, these two matrices are fairly close to each other.
To see this, we write

\bigg(I-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\bigg)^{-2}=I+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}J_{t}(x_{t})+A,

where
A
is a matrix obeying (see (
222
))

\|A\|\lesssim\left\|\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\right\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{d^{2}\log^{4}T}{T^{2}}.

Consequently, we can demonstrate that

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\bigg\|\bigg(I-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\bigg)^{-1}\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big)\bigg\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\left\{\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\bigg(I+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}J_{t}(x_{t})\bigg)\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big)\right\}+O\bigg(\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\|A\|\,\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big)^{\top}\bigg(I+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}J_{t}(x_{t})\bigg)\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big)+O\bigg(\frac{d^{3}\log^{5}T}{T^{2}}\bigg).

To see why the last line holds, note that (according to Lemma
1

and the properties (
39
))

\displaystyle\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big\|_{2}+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\big\|\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}\,|\,X_{t}=x_{t}\right]\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}+\sqrt{\frac{d\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}}\left(1-\alpha_{t}\right)\asymp\sqrt{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T},

and hence

\frac{\alpha_{t}}{1-\alpha_{t}}\|A\|\,\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\frac{d^{3}\log^{5}T}{T^{2}}.

Combining the above bound with (
224
), we
arrive at

p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})=f_{9}(x_{t})\exp\Big(-f_{10}(x_{t},x_{t-1})+\zeta_{t,5}(x_{t},x_{t-1})\Big)

(225a)

for some function
f_{9}(\cdot)
, where

\displaystyle f_{10}(x_{t},x_{t-1})

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\alpha_{t})}\bigg\|\bigg(I-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\bigg)^{-1}\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big)\bigg\|_{2}^{2},

(225b)

\displaystyle\big|\zeta_{t,5}(x_{t},x_{t-1})\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d^{3}\log^{4.5}T}{T^{3/2}}.

(225c)

To finish up, repeat Step 3 in the proof of Lemma
8
to yield

f_{7}(x_{t})=\left(1+O\left(\frac{d^{3}\log^{4.5}T}{T^{3/2}}\right)\right)\frac{1}{\big(2\pi\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\big)^{d/2}\big|\det\big(I-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\big)\big|}

as claimed. This combined with (
225
) concludes the proof.

E.2.1
Proof of auxiliary claims in Lemma
12

Proof of relation (
216
).

For any
(x_{t},x_{t-1})\in\mathcal{E}
, one necessarily has

\displaystyle\|x_{t}(\gamma)-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}\leq\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}\leq c_{3}\sqrt{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}.

(226)

Given
x_{t}
, we define the set

\mathcal{E}_{1}\coloneqq\big\{x:\|\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x\|_{2}\leq c_{4}\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})\log T}\big\}.

Then for any
x_{0}\in\mathcal{E}_{1}
, one has

\displaystyle\big\|x_{t}(\gamma)-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\|\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\|_{2},\|\widehat{x}_{t}-x_{t-1}\|_{2}\right\}\leq\max\left\{c_{4}\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})\log T},c_{3}\sqrt{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}\right\}

\displaystyle=c_{4}\sqrt{d(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})\log T},

where the last inequality comes from (
39b
).
This in turn reveals that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\,|\,x_{0}\big)}{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}(\widehat{x}_{t}\,|\,x_{0})}-1\right|=\left|\exp\bigg(\frac{\big\|\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}-\frac{\big\|x_{t}(\gamma)-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}\bigg)-1\right|

\displaystyle\quad\quad\leq\left|\exp\bigg(\frac{\big\|\widehat{x}_{t}-x_{t}(\gamma)\big\|_{2}\big\{\big\|\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}+\big\|x_{t}(\gamma)-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}\big\}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}\bigg)-1\right|

\displaystyle\quad\quad\lesssim\frac{\big\|\widehat{x}_{t}-x_{t}(\gamma)\big\|_{2}\big\{\big\|\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}+\big\|x_{t}(\gamma)-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}\big\}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim d\sqrt{\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}}\log T\lesssim d\sqrt{\frac{\log^{3}T}{T}}=o(1),

where the second line follows from the elementary relation

\Big|\|a\|_{2}^{2}-\|b\|_{2}^{2}\Big|=\Big|\|a\|_{2}-\|b\|_{2}\Big|\cdot\left(\|a\|_{2}+\|b\|_{2}\right)\leq\|a-b\|_{2}\left(\|a\|_{2}+\|b\|_{2}\right),

and the last line relies on (
39
) and our assumption on
T
.
Moreover, repeating the same argument as in (
116
) and (
120
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{p_{X_{t-1}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\big)}{p_{X_{t-1}}(\widehat{x}_{t})}

\displaystyle=1+O\bigg(d\sqrt{\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}}\log T\bigg).

(227)

Putting the above results together leads to

\displaystyle\frac{p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}(\gamma)\big)}{p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t})}

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\,|\,x_{0}\big)/p_{X_{t-1}}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\big)}{p_{X_{t-1}\,|\,X_{0}}(\widehat{x}_{t}\,|\,x_{0})/p_{X_{t-1}}(\widehat{x}_{t})}=1+O\bigg(d\sqrt{\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}}\log T\bigg).

Equipped with the above relation, we can demonstrate that

\displaystyle\Big\|\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}(\gamma)\big)\big(x_{t}(\gamma)-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}-\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t})\big(\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq O\Big(d\sqrt{\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}}\log T\Big)\cdot\bigg(\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}(\gamma)\big)\big\|x_{t}(\gamma)-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Big\|\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\big(x_{t}(\gamma)-\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}\Big\|_{2}

(228)

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\sqrt{d^{3}(1-\alpha_{t})\log^{3}T},

(229)

where the last step invokes the property (
43a
).
Following similar arguments (which we omit for brevity), we can also derive

\displaystyle\Big\|\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}(\gamma)\big)\big(x_{t}(\gamma)-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big)\big(x_{t}(\gamma)-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}\mathrm{d}x_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad-\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t})\big(\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big)\big(\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}\mathrm{d}x_{0}\Big\|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim d^{2}\sqrt{(1-\alpha_{t})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})}\log^{2}T,

(230)

where we have made use of the property (
43b
).
Taking the above two above perturbation bounds together with the expression (
94
) and making use of (
43a
) immediately lead to the advertised result:

\displaystyle\big\|J_{t-1}\big(x_{t}(\gamma)\big)-J_{t-1}(\widehat{x}_{t})\big\|\lesssim d^{2}\sqrt{\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}}\log^{2}T.

(231)

Proof of relation (
217
).

To establish this relation, we first apply the triangle inequality:

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{J_{t-1}(\widehat{x}_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}-\frac{J_{t}(x_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\frac{J_{t-1}(\widehat{x}_{t})-J_{t}(x_{t})}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\bigg\|+\bigg\|\bigg(\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg)J_{t}(x_{t})\bigg\|.

Let us first consider the second term

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bigg(\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg)J_{t}(x_{t})\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}(1-\alpha_{t})}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\|J_{t}(x_{t})\|\leq\frac{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}(1-\alpha_{t})d\log T}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(1-\alpha_{t})d\log T}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}}.

(232)

where the last inequality uses (
161a
) and the properties (
39
).

Next, we move on to bound the difference
J_{t-1}(\widehat{x}_{t})-J_{t}(x_{t})
.
By virtue of the relation (
C.1.1
), one can deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\big(\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,{x}_{t}\big)\big({x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}\left\|\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}-\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,{x}_{t}\big)\big({x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}O\bigg(\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\bigg)\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,{x}_{t}\big)\big\|{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}.

In addition, recognizing that
\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}}=1+\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{t}}(1+\sqrt{\alpha_{t}})}=1+O(1-\alpha_{t})
,
we can further invoke the triangle inequality and (
39a
) to obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\big(\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}-\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,{x}_{t}\big)\big({x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\mathrm{d}x_{0}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,{x}_{t}\big)\big\|{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}.

Repeating the same argument also reveals that

\displaystyle\left\|\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t-1}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,\widehat{x}_{t}\big)\big(\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big)\big(\widehat{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}\mathrm{d}x_{0}-\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}\big)\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}\mathrm{d}x_{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\left\|\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}\big)\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}\mathrm{d}x_{0}\right\|.

In view of the expression (
94
) for
J_{t}
, combining the preceding two bounds with a little algebra yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}\big\|J_{t-1}(\widehat{x}_{t})-J_{t}(x_{t})\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\frac{d(1-\alpha_{t})\log T}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\Bigg\{\bigg\|\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,x_{t}\big)\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)\big(x_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big)^{\top}\mathrm{d}x_{0}\bigg\|+\left(\int p_{X_{0}\,|\,X_{t}}\big(x_{0}\,|\,{x}_{t}\big)\big\|{x}_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}x_{0}\big\|_{2}\mathrm{d}x_{0}\right)^{2}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{d^{2}(1-\alpha_{t})\log^{2}T}{(\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t})^{2}},

where the last line follows from Lemma
1
and the properties (
39
).

Putting the above bounds together immediately establishes relation (
217
).

E.3
Proof of Lemma
13

According to the expression (
209
), one has

Y_{t-1}\mid Y_{t}=x_{t}\ \sim\ \mathcal{N}\Bigg(\mu_{t}(x_{t}),\,\underset{\eqqcolon\,\Sigma(\widehat{x}_{t})}{\underbrace{\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\bigg(I-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}J_{t}(x_{t})\bigg)^{2}}}\,\Bigg).

In order to quantify the density, we first bound the Jacobian matrix
J_{t}(x)
defined in (
37
).
On the one hand, the expression (
38
) tells us that
J_{t}({x})\preceq I_{d}
for any
x
,
given that the term within the curly bracket in (
38
) is a negative covariance matrix.
On the other hand,
J_{t}(x)
can be lower bounded by

\displaystyle J_{t}(x)

\displaystyle\succeq-\frac{1}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big(X_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big)\big(X_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big)^{\top}\mid X_{t}=x\Big]

\displaystyle\succeq-\frac{\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\|X_{t}-\sqrt{\overline{\alpha}_{t}}X_{0}\big\|_{2}^{2}\mid X_{t}=x\Big]}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}I_{d}\succeq-\frac{2\|x\|_{2}^{2}+2T^{2c_{R}}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}I_{d}

\displaystyle\succeq-T^{c_{0}+1}\big(\|x\|_{2}^{2}+T^{2c_{R}}\big)I_{d},

where the second line applies the assumption that
\|X_{0}\|_{2}\leq T^{c_{R}}
,
and the last line invokes the choice (
22
).
As a consequence, we have

\displaystyle\Sigma(\widehat{x}_{t})

\displaystyle\succeq\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}\bigg(1-\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{2(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})}\bigg)^{2}I_{d}=\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{4\alpha_{t}}\bigg(\frac{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}+\alpha_{t}-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}{1-\overline{\alpha}_{t}}\bigg)^{2}I_{d}\succeq\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{4\alpha_{t}}I_{d}\succeq\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{4}I_{d};

(233a)

\displaystyle\Sigma(\widehat{x}_{t})

\displaystyle\preceq\frac{1-\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t}}T^{2c_{0}+2}\big(2\|\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{4}+2T^{4c_{R}}\big)I_{d}\preceq 4T^{2c_{0}+2}\big(\|\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{4}+T^{4c_{R}}\big)I_{d}.

(233b)

With the above relations in mind, we are ready to bound the density function
p_{Y_{t-1}\,|\,Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})
for any
x_{t},x_{t-1}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
.
It is seen from (
209
) that

\displaystyle\log\frac{1}{p_{Y_{t-1}\mid Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big){}^{\top}\big(\Sigma(\widehat{x}_{t})\big)^{-1}\big(x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big)}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\log\mathsf{det}\big(\Sigma(\widehat{x}_{t})\big)+\frac{d}{2}\log(2\pi)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\big\|x_{t-1}-\mu_{t}(x_{t})\big\|_{2}^{2}}{1-\alpha_{t}}+\frac{d}{2}\log\Big(8\pi T^{2c_{0}+2}\big(\|\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{4}+T^{4c_{R}}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq 2T^{c_{0}+1}\left\{2\big\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big\|_{2}^{2}+\|x_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+T^{2c_{R}}\right\}+\frac{d}{2}\log\Big(8\pi T^{2c_{0}+2}\big(\|\widehat{x}_{t}\|_{2}^{4}+T^{4c_{R}}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq T^{c_{0}+2c_{R}+2}\left\{\big\|x_{t-1}-\widehat{x}_{t}\big\|_{2}^{2}+\|x_{t}\|_{2}^{2}+1\right\},

where the second inequality results from (
233
),
and the third inequality makes use of (
177
) and (
22
).
Given that
\log\frac{p_{X_{t-1}\mid X_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}{p_{Y_{t-1}\mid Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}\leq\log\frac{1}{p_{Y_{t-1}\mid Y_{t}}(x_{t-1}\,|\,x_{t})}
,
we have concluded the proof.
